MANDURA

MAṆḌŪRA

Definition
These are preparations containing Śodhita Maṇḍūra along with other drugs.
Method of preparation
Maṇḍūra is purified by a special method (the text is given under the first preparation)
and boiled in Gomūtra till it becomes a rasakriy ā (a paste). Then the powders mentioned in
the yogas are added and stirred well. While warm, Vaṭakas are prepared. This can be kept in
powder form also.
Characteristics and preservation
These emit a strong smell of Gomūtra and are dark in colour. These preserve their potency
indefinitely. These should be kept away from moisture.
1 PUNARNAVĀDI MAṆḌŪRA Carakasaṃhitā, Cikitsāsthāna, Adhyāya 16 93-95
2 MAṆḌŪRA VAṬAKA Aṣṭāṅgahṛdaya, Cikitsāsthāna, Adhyāya 16 16-19 1/2

1. Lohamala (Maṇḍūra) Q.S.

2. Bibhītakaṅgāra (Bibhītaka) (St.) Q.S. for heat
3. Gomūtra Q.S. for Pariśecana
Special Method of Preparation
Maṇḍūra should be boiled in Gomūtra. When it becomes thick and Pasty, other C
ūrṇas are added and stirred well and pills are prepared.
Dose
1 g
Anupāna
Buttermilk, water
Important Therapeutic Uses
Pāṇḍu Roga (Anaemia), Grahaṇī (Malabsorption syndrome), Śotha (Inflammation),
Plīhā Roga (Splenic disease), Viṣamajvara (Intermittent fever), Arśa (Haemorrhoids),
Kuṣṭha (Diseases of skin), Kṛmi (Helminthiasis/Worm infestation)

MAṆḌŪRA VAṬAKA

(Aṣṭāṅgahṛdaya, Cikitsāsthāna, Adhyāya 16: 16-19 1/2)

1. Tāpya (Mākṣika) bhasma 1 Part
2. Dārvī (Dāruharidrā) (St.) 1 Part
3. Cavya (St.) 1 Part
4. Granthika (Pippalīmūla) (Rt.) 1 Part
5. Devadāru (Ht. Wd.) 1 Part
6. Nāgarā (Śuṇthī) (Rz.) 1 Part
7. Marica (Fr.) 1 Part
8. Pippalī (Fr.) 1 Part
9. Agni (Citraka) (Rt.) 1 Part

12. Dhātrī (Āmalakī) (P.) 1 Part
13. Bibhītaka (P.) 1 Part
14. Mustā (Rz.) 1 Part
15. Maṇḍūra bhasma 28 Parts
16. Gomūtra 336 Parts
Special Method of Preparation
Fine powder of Shodhita Mandura is taken.
Dose
1 g.
Anupāna
Buttermilk, Honey
Pathya, Apathya
Rice and Butter milk
Important Therapeutic Uses
Pāṇḍu (Anaemia), Aruci (Tastelessness), Śotha (Inflammation), Ūrustambha
(Stiffness in thigh muscles), Prameha (Urinary disorders), Arśa (Haemorrhoids), Kuṣṭha
(Diseases of skin), Ajīrṇa (Dyspepsia), Kāmalā (Jaundice), Plīhā Roga (Splenic disease)

RASA YOGA

RASA YOGA

Definition
Preparations containing mineral drugs as main ingredients are called Rasa Yogas.
They may be in pill form. They are mixed and triturated together.
Method of preparation
Drugs such as Abhraka, Mākṣika, Svarṇa, Rajata, Tāmra, Kāṃsya etc. are used only
in bhasma form in these preparations. Drugs such as Gandhaka, Manaḥśila etc. are used in
purified form. Where Rasa and Gandhaka are drugs, Kajjalī is prepared first with these two
and then only other drugs are added in small quantities and ground in the khalva itself and
mixed well. Bhāvanā with the prescribed Svarasa, Kvātha etc. should be given to this for a
prescribed period.
Charateristics and preservation
The colour and smell depend on the drugs in the yogas. They keep their potency
indefinitely, unless otherwise prescribed.

2. Mṛtābhra (Abhraka) - bhasma 1 Part

4. Pathyā (Harītakī) (P.) 3 Parts

250 to 500 mg
Anupāna
Honey
Important Therapeutic Uses
Amlapitta (Dyspepsia)

AŚVAKAṄCUKĪ RASA

(Rasayogasāgara, 66: 1065-67)

1. Pārada-śuddha 1 Part
2. Taṅkaṇa-śuddha 1 Part
3. Gandhaka - śuddha 1 Part
4. Viṣa (Vatsanābha) - śuddha (Rt. Tr.) 1 Part

12. Jayapāla -śuddha (Enm.) 1 Part

The drugs are put in a khalva and Bhāvanā is done for 21 days with Bhṛṅgarāja
Svarasa and made into pills and dried in shade.

125 mg
Anupāna
Honey, Ārdraka Rasa, Tāmbūla Rasa, cold water
Important Therapeutic Uses

Śvāsa (Dyspnoea/Asthma), Kāsa (Cough), Ānāha (Distension of abdomen due to
obstruction to passage of urine and stools), Vibandha (Constipation), Jvara (Fever)

(Yogaratnākara; Mūtrakṛcchracikitsā: Page 543)

1. Sūtaka (Pārada) - śuddha 12 g
2. Tāmra - bhasma 12 g
3. Abhraka bhasma 12 g
4. Gandhaka - śuddha 24 g
5. Mustā - kvātha (Rz.) Q.S. bhavana 7 times
6. Dāḍima- svarasa (Fr.) Q.S. bhavana 7 times
7. Dūrvā - svarasa (Pl.) Q.S. bhavana 7 times
8. Ketakī - svarasa (Rt.) Q.S. bhavana 7 times
9. Stanaja drava (Godugdha) Q.S. bhavana 7 times
10. Sahadevī (Pl.) Q.S. bhavana 7 times
11. Kumārī (Lf.) Q.S. bhavana 7 times
12. Parpaṭa (Pl.) Q.S. bhavana 7 times
13. Rāmaśītalikā- toya (Pl.) Q.S. bhavana 7 times
14. Śatāvarī - rasa (Rt. Tr.) Q.S. bhavana 7 times
15. Tiktā (Kaṭukā) (Rt./Rz.) 12 g
16. Guḍūcī sattva (Guḍūcī) (St.) 12 g
17. Parpaṭa (Pl.) 12 g
18. Uśīra (Rt.) 12 g
19. Mādhavī (Fl.) 12 g
20. Śrīgandha (Śvetacandana) (Ht. Wd.) 12 g
21. Sāriva (Śveta Sārivā) (Rt.) 12 g
22. Drākṣā - kaṣāya (Dr. Fr.) Q.S. bhavana 7 times
Special Method of Preparation
First Kajjalī of Pārada and Gandhaka is prepared. Drug 2&3 are added to it and
seven Bhāvanas With drugs 5 to 14 are given separately. Thereafter powders of drugs 15 to

21 are added to it and Bhāvana given with Drākṣā kaṣāya for seven times.
Important Therapeutic Uses
All Pittaroga (All diseases due to Pitta doṣa), Vātapitta Roga (Disease due to Vāta
doṣa and Pitta doṣa), Antardāha (Internal Burning sensation), Bāhyadāha (Burning
sensation ), Mahātāpa (Severe body temperature), Jvara (Fever), Bhrama (Vertigo), Mūtrak
ṛcchra (Dysuria), Raktapradara (Menorrhagia or Metrorrhagia or both), Ūrdhvagata
Raktapitta (Bleeding from orifices of the upper part of the body), Raktavami (Haematemesis)

The paste is covered with Eraṇḍa Leaves and kept for three days within a heap of

125 mg

Bhrama (Vertigo), Apasmāra (Epilepsy), Vātaroga (Disease due to Vāta doṣa), Dhātu

Kāsa (Cough), Jīrṇajvara (Chronic fever), Rājayakṣmā (Tuberculosis), Kṣaya
(Pthisis)

MṚTYUÑJAYA RASA

(Bhaiṣajyaratnāvalī, Jvarādhikāra: 409-418)
1. Viṣa (Vatsanābha) go mūtra śodhita (Rt.Tr.) 1 Part
2. Marica (Fr.) 1 Part
3. Pippalī (Kaṇā,) (Pippalī) (Fr.) 1 Part
4. Gandhaka - śuddha 1 Part
5. Taṅkaṇa - śuddha 1 Part

6. Hiṅgula - jambīra rasa śodhita 2 Parts
Or
Rasa (Pārada) - śuddha 1 Part
Dose
125 mg
Anupāna
Mastu, Ārdraka Svarasa, Honey
Important Therapeutic Uses
Sarva Jvara (All types of fevers), Viṣamajvara (Intermittent fever), Jīrṇajvara
(Chronic fever)

YOGENDRA RASA

(Bhaiṣajyaratnāvalī, Vātavyādhyadhikāra: 506-512)

1. Rasa Sindūra (Pārada) 1 Part
2. Hāṭaka (Svarṇa) bhasma 1/2 Part
3. Kāntaloha bhasma (Lauha) 1/2 Part
4. Abhra (Abhraka) bhasma 1/2 Part
5. Mauktika (Muktā) bhasma 1/2 Part
6. Vaṅga bhasma 1/2 Part
7. Kumārī rasa (Lf.) Q.S. (for mardana)
Special Method of Preparation
Sl. No.1-6 to be placed in a kharala for bhāvanā and Mardana With Kumārī Svarasa
and then make pellets and dry, then whole thing covered with Eraṇḍa, leaves and kept in a
heap of Dhānya for 3 days
Dose
125 to 250 mg
Anupāna
Triphalā Kvātha, Honey
Important Therapeutic Uses
Bahumūtra (Polyuria), Prameha (Urinary disorders), Vātaroga (Disease due to Vāta
doṣa), Apasmāra (Epilepsy), Pakṣāghāta (Paralysis/Hemiplegia), Bhagandara (Fistula-in-
ano), Arśa (Haemorrhoids), Vātapitta Roga (Disease due to Vāta doṣa and Pitta doṣa), M
ūtrāghāta (Urinary obstruction), Guda Roga (Anorectal disease), Mūrcchā (Syncope),
Unmāda (Mania/Psychosis), Yakṣmā (Tuberculosis), Kṣīṇendriya (Impaired senses), Śūla
(Colicky Pain), Amlapitta (Dyspepsia)

RATNAGIRI RASA

(Bhaiṣajyaratnāvalī, Jvarādhikāra: 433-438)

1. Śuddha sūta (Pārada) 1 Part
2. Gandha (Gandhaka) - śuddha 1 Part
3. Mṛta Tāmra (Tāmra) - bhasma 1 Part
4. Mṛta Abhra (Abhraka) - bhasma 1 Part
5. Mṛta Hāṭaka (Svarṇa) - bhasma 1 Part
6. Mṛta Lauhaka (Lauha) - bhasma 1/2 Part
7. Mṛta Vaikrānta (Vaikrānta) - bhasma 1/4 Part
8. Bhṛṅgarāja drava (Bhṛṅgarāja) (Pl.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times
9. Śigru - drava- rasa (Lf.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times
10. Vāsaka (Vāsā) drava (Lf.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times
11. Nirguṇḍī - drava (Lf.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times

12. Vacā - drava (Rz.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times
13. Agni drava (Citraka) (Rt.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times
14. Bhṛṅga drava (Bhṛṅgarāja) (Pl.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times
15. Muṇḍīka- drava (Mūṇḍītikā) (Rt.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times
16. Drākṣā - drava (Dr.Fr.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times
17. Amṛtā drava (Guḍūcī) (St.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times
18. Jayantī drava (Lf.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times
19. Muni drava (Agastya) (Lf.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times
20. Brāhmī - drava (Pl.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times
21. Sutiktaka drava (Kaṭukā) (Rz.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times
22. Kanyā drava (Kumārī) (Lf.) Q.S. for bhāvanā 3
times
Special Method of Preparation
Kajjalī is melted as is done for Parpaṭī and mixed with other drugs. Mardana with
Bhṛṅgarāja Svarasa and Bhāvanā with other drugs 9 to 22 is done one after another. Then
Laghu Puṭa in Vālukā Yantra is given.
Dose
125 mg

Anupāna
Pippalī Cūrṇa, Dhānyaka Cūrṇa, Honey
Important Therapeutic Uses
Navajvara (Acute fever)

RASAMĀṆIKYA

(Siddhabhaiṣajyamaṇimāla, Jvaraprakaraṇa: 66)
1. Tāla - śuddha (Haratāla) Q.S.
Special Method of Preparation
Small pieces of Tāla (Haratāla) are kept on Abhraka (Mica) Sheet and covered by
another sheet of Abhraka. This Abhraka Sampuṭa Containing Tālaka is kept on moderate
fire. When Tālaka is melted and colour is also changed like Māṇikya (Red) Sampuṭa
Dose
125 to 250 mg
Anupāna
Ghee, honey
Important Therapeutic Uses
Vātaśleṣma Jvara (Fever due to Vāta doṣa and Śleṣma doṣa), Śvāsa
(Dyspnoea/Asthma)

RASARĀJA RASA

(Bhaiṣajyaratnāvalī, Vātavyādhyadhikāra: 470-476)

1. Mūrcchita Sūta - Rasa sindhūra (Pārada) 48 g
2. Vyoma sattva (Abhraka) - bhasma 12 g
3. Suvarṇa (Svarṇa) - bhasma 12 g
4. Kanyā rasa (Kumārī) (Lf.) Q.S. (for bhāvanā
& Mardana)
5. Mṛta lauha (Lauha) bhasma 6 g
6. Mṛta rūpya (Rajata) - bhasma 6 g
7. Mṛta Vaṅga (Vaṅga) bhasma 6 g
8. Vājigandhā (Aśvagandhā) (Rt.) 6 g
9. Lavaṅgaka (Lavaṅga) (Fl. Bd.) 6 g
10. Jātīkoṣa (Jātīphala) (Ar.) 6 g
11. Kṣīrakākolī (Sub. Rt.) 6 g
12. Kākamācī rasa (Pl.) Q.S.(for bhāvanā
& mardhana)

Dose
125 to 250 mg
Anupāna
Honey, milk, sugar water
Important Therapeutic Uses
Pakṣāghāta (Paralysis/Hemiplegia), Ardita (Facial palsy), Apatantraka (Hysteria with
loss of consciousness), Apatānaka (Tetanic convulsions), Hanustambha (Lock jaw), Bādhirya
(Deafness), Mastaka Bhrama (Vertigo), Vātaroga (Disease due to Vāta doṣa), Daurbalya
(Weakness), Śukrakṣaya (Oligospermia)

RĀJAMṚGĀṄKA RASA

(Rasendrasārasaṅgraha, Yakṣmācikitsā: 3 - 7)
1. Rasa bhasma (Pārada) - Rasa sindūra 3 Parts
2. Hema (Svarṇa) bhasma 1 Part
3. Mṛta Tāra (Rajata) bhasma 1 Part

4. Śilā (Manaḥśilā) - śuddha 2 Parts
5. Gandhaka - śuddha 2 Parts
6. Tālaka (Haritāla) -śuddha 2 Parts
7. Varāṭikā (Kapardika) -śuddha 1 Part
8. Taṅkaṇa - śuddha 1 Part
Special Method of Preparation
Drugs 1 to 6 are mixed together Varāṭikās are filled with the powder and the mouths
of Varāṭikās are sealed by a paste made of Taṅkaṇa and Ajā Kṣīra.These Varāṭikas are
placed in Śarāva Sampuṭa and sealed with clay smeared cloth. This is heated in Ga
Dose
250 to 500 mg
Anupāna
Pippalī Cūrṇa And Honey, Marica Cūrṇa And Ghṛta
Important Therapeutic Uses
Vātaśleṣmajakṣaya (Pthisis due to Vāta doṣa and Śleṣma doṣa)

LAGHU MĀLINĪVASANTA RASA

(Yogaratnākara, Jvarādhikāra: Page 211)

1. Rasaka (Kharpara) - bhasma 2 Parts
2. Vallija nistuṣa (Marica) (Fr.) 1 Part
3. Mṛkṣaṇa (Navanīta) Q.S. (for mardana)
4. Nimbū nīra (Nimbū) (Fr.) Q.S. (for mardana)
Special Method of Preparation
To be ground with Nimbū Svarasa till the stickyness is removed from the medicine.
Note: Since the genuine Kharpara bhasma not generally available Yaśada bhasma which has
the same property is used in place of Kharpara bhasma.
Dose
250 mg
Anupāna
Milk, honey
Important Therapeutic Uses
Pittavikāra (Disorder of Pitta doṣa), Raktavikāra (Disorders of blood), Jvara (Fever),
Kṣaya (Pthisis), Pradara (Excessive vaginal discharge), Garbhiṇī Roga (Disorders during
Pregnancy), Arśa (Haemorrhoids), Jīrṇajvara (Chronic fever), Dhātugata Atīsāra (Diarrhoea
resulting in tissue wasting), Netraroga (Eye disorder), Raktārśa (Bleeding haemorrhoids),
Viṣamajvara (Intermittent fever)

LAGHVĀNANDA RASA

(Rasendrasārasaṅgraha, Pāṇḍukāmalācikitsā: 44-46)

1. Pārada - śuddha 1 Part
2. Gandhaka - śuddha 1 Part
3. Lauha bhasma 1 Part
4. Viṣa (Vatsanābha) - śuddha (Rt.Tr.) 1 Part
5. Abhraka bhasma 1 Part
6. Marica (Fr.) 8 Parts
7. Taṅgaṇa - śuddha (Taṅkaṇa) bhasma 4 Parts
8. Bhṛṅgarāja (Pl.) Q.S. (for bhavana &
mardana) 7 times
9. Amlavetasa (Fr.) Q.S. (for bhavana &
mardana) 7 times
Dose
250 mg
Anupāna
Betel leaf and honey
Important Therapeutic Uses
Agnimāndya (Digestive impairment), Vāta Kapha Roga (Disease due to Vāta doṣa
and Kapha doṣa), Pāṇḍu (Anaemia), Jvara (Fever), Bhrama (Vertigo), Pradara (Excessive
vaginal discharge), Aruci (Tastelessness), Gulma (Abdominal lump)

LAKṢMĪNĀRĀYAṆA RASA

(Yogaratnākara,Vātavyādhicikitsā: Page 470)

1. Gandhaka - śuddha 1 Part
2. Taṅkaṇa - śuddha 1 Part
3. Viṣa (Vatsanābha) - śuddha (Rt. Tr.) 1 Part
4. Hiṅgula-śuddha 1 Part
5. Rohiṇī (Kaṭukā) (Rz.) 1 Part
6. Ativiṣā - śuddha (Rt. Tr.) 1 Part
7. Kṛṣnā (pippalī) (Fr.) 1 Part
8. Vatsaka (Kuṭaja) (Sd.) 1 Part
9. Abhraka bhasma 1 Part
10. Saindhava lavaṇa 1 Part
11. Dantī drava (Rt) Q.S.(for bhavana & mardana 3)
days
12. Harītakī (P.) Q.S.(for bhavana & mardana 3)
days
13. Āmalakī (P.) Q.S.(for bhavana & mardana 3)
days

14. Bibhītaka (P.) Q.S.(for bhavana & mardana 3)
days
Dose
250 mg
Anupāna
Ārdraka Svarasa, Tāmbūla Rasa, Nārīkelodaka
Important Therapeutic Uses
Prasūtavāta (Puerperal disorder), Vātaroga (Disease due to Vāta doṣa), Jvara (Fever),
Sūtikāroga (Post partum disorder puerperal disorders), Sannipāta Roga (Disease due to
vitiation of all Doṣa), Viṣamajvara (Intermittent fever), Viṣūcī (Gastro-enteritis with
piercing pain), Atīsāra (Diarrhoea), Grahaṇī (Malabsorption syndrome), Raktātīsāra
(Diarrhoea with Bleeding ), Āmātīsāra (Diarrhoea due to indigestion/ Āma), Meha
(Excessive flow of urine), Śūla (Colicky Pain)

LAKṢMĪVILĀSA RASA (NĀRADĪYA)

(Bhaiṣajyaratnāvalī, Rasāyanādhikara: 55-68)

1. Kṛṣṇābhra (Abhraka) bhasma 48 g
2. Rasa (Pārada) - śuddha 24 g
3. Gandhaka - śuddha 24 g
4. Candra (Karpūra) (Sub. Ext.) 12 g
5. Jātikośa (Jātīphala) (Ar.) 12 g
6. Jātīphala (Sd.) 12 g
7. Vṛddhadāraka (Vṛddhadāruka) (Sd.) 12 g
8. Dhustūraka (Dhattūra) (Sd.) 12 g
9. Trailokya vijayā (Vijayā) -bīja (Bhaṅga) (Sd.) 12 g
10. Vidārī mūla (Rt. Tr.) 12 g

11. Nārāyaṇī (Śatavari) (Rt. Tr.) 12 g
12. Nāgabalā (Rt.) 12 g
13. Atibalā (Rt.) 12 g
14. Gokṣuraka (Gokṣura) (Fr.) 12 g
15. Nicula - bīja (Sd.) 12 g
16. Parṇa patra (Nāgavallī) (Lf.) Q.S. (for mardana)
Dose
250 mg
Anupāna
Tāmbūla Svarasa, honey, milk
Important Therapeutic Uses
Udara (Diseases of abdomen / enlargement of abdomen), Prameha (Urinary
disorders), Dhātu Kṣaya (Tissue wasting), Ūrdhvāṅga Roga (Disordrs of Head and brain),
Nāḍīvraṇa (Fistula), Kuṣṭha (Diseases of skin), Vraṇa (Ulcer), Guda Roga (Anorectal
disease), Bhagandara (Fistula-in-ano), All kinds of Ślīpada (Filariasis), Galaśoṣa (Dryness in
throat), Āntra Vṛddhi (Hernia), Atīsāra (Diarrhoea), Āmavāta (Rheumatism), Jihvāstambha
(Glossal palsy), Galagraha (Difficulty in swallowing), Mukha-Kaṛṇa-Nāsa-Akṣi Vikṛti
(Disorders of Mouth, Ear, Nose and Eyes), Kāsa (Cough), Pīnasa (Chronic rhinitis/sinusitis),
Yakṣmā (Tuberculosis), Arśa (Haemorrhoids), Sthaulya (Obesity)

LOKANĀTHA RASA

(Rasendrasāra Saṇgraha, Plīha Cikitsā: 2 - 5)

1. Pārada-śuddha 12 g
2. Gandhaka - śuddha 12 g
3. Mṛtābhra (Abhraka) - bhasma 12 g
4. Loha bhasma 24 g
5. Tāmraka (Tāmra) - bhasma 24 g
6. Varāṭikā (Kapardikā) bhasma 72 g
7. Nāgavallī rasa (Lf.) Q.S. (for mardana)
Special Method of Preparation
Drugs 1 to 6 are mixed well and mardana is done in Nāgavallī Rasa and small
cakrikās are prepared.These cakrikās are kept in Śarāva Sampuṭa and Gajapuṭa is given.
When cool, the cakrikas are powdered and bottled.
Dose
125-250 mg
Anupāna
Honey, Gomūtra
Important Therapeutic Uses
Yakṛtplīhodara (Disease of liver and spleen), Gulma (Abdominal lump), Śotha
(Inflammation)

VASANTA MĀLATĪ RASA

(Siddhabhaiṣajyamaṇimāla, Jvaraprakaraṇa: 60-62)

1. Svarṇa - bhasma 1 Part
2. Muktā bhasma 2 Parts
3. Darada (Hiṅgula) -śuddha 3 Parts
4. Marica (Fr.) 4 Parts
5. Kharpara bhasma 8 Parts
6. Navanīta 18/16 or 1 1/8 Part
7. Nimbuka (Nimbū) svarasa (Fr.) Q.S. (for mardana)
Note: 1.Yaśada bhasma may be used in place of Kharpara bhasma.
2.Usually Nimbū svarasa is taken four times the weight of drugs.
The mardana is continued till the greasiness of Navaavanīta ends.
Dose
125 to 250 mg
Anupāna
Pippalī Cūrṇa, Honey
Important Therapeutic Uses

Jīrṇa Kāsa (Chronic cough), Viṣamajvara (Intermittent fever), Jīrṇajvara (Chronic
fever)

(Rasendrasārasaṅgraha, Rasāyanavājīkaraṇādhikāra: 80-85)
1. Hāṭaka (Svarṇa) bhasma 2 Parts
2. Candra (Rajata) bhasma 2 Parts

7. Pravāla bhasma 4 Parts

mardana)

mardana)
11. Vāsā (Lf.) Q.S. (for bhāvanā &
mardana)
12. Lākṣā (Gl.) Q.S. (for bhāvanā
& mardana)
13. Udīcya (Rt.) Q.S. (for bhāvanā &
mardana)
14. Rambhā kanda (Kadalī) (Rt. Tr.) Q.S. (for bhāvanā &
mardana)
15. Śata patra (Śatapatrikā) (Fl.) Q.S. (for bhāvanā &
mardana)
16. Mālatī (Jātī) (Fl.) Q.S. (for bhāvanā &
mardana)
17. Mṛgamada 1 Part (for bhāvanā &

Special Method of Preparation
Drugs 1 to 8 are mixed well and Bhāvanas in drugs 9 to 16 are given separately one
after the other.Kastūrī is mixed in water and Bhāvanā in Kastūri Jala is given. It is ground
well and stored in well stoppered bottle.
Dose
125 to 250 mg
Anupāna
Honey, Goghṛta, Milk, Sitā
Important Therapeutic Uses
Meha (Excessive flow of urine), Valī Palita (Wrinkles in skin and graying of hair),

Smṛti Bhraṃśa (Impairment of memory), Kṣaya (Pthisis), Somaroga (Polyuria in females),
Kārśya (Emaciation)

Sampuṭa and Pāka in Bālukā Yantra for 24 hours is done. The drug should be re
Dose
62.5 to 125 mg
Anupāna

Important Therapeutic Uses
Grahaṇī (Malabsorption syndrome), Pāṇḍu (Anaemia), Jalodara (Ascites), Śotha
(Inflammation), Urastoya (Pleural effusion/ Hydrothorax), Kuṣṭha (Diseases of skin),

ulcer/Duodenal ulcer/Colic), Udara (Diseases of abdomen / enlargement of abdomen),

LAUHA

LAUHA

Definition
Lauha kalpas are preparations of Lauha Bhasma as main ingredient added to other
drugs.
Method of preparation
The drugs are reduced to fine powder and mixed with Lauha Bhasma. Bhāvanā is
given with prescribed liquids if mentioned.
Characteristics and preservation
The powder should be very fine and the bhasma used should be well prepared. When
well protected from moisture and heat, they keep their potency for a period of two years.
Preparations containing mercury or its compounds keep their potency indefinitely.

PUṬAPAKVA VIṢAMA JVARĀNTAKA LAUHA

(Rasendrasārasangraha, Jvarādhikāra : 271-277)
1. Hiṅgula sambhava Sūta (Pārada) śuddha 4 Parts

Drugs of S.No. 1 & 2 are mixed and parpati to be made and then all drugs are mixed
together and placed in the Sampuṭa of Muktā Śukti after drying and Laghu Puṭa is given.
After the Puṭa, The shells are removed and then Bhasma alone is taken.

250 mg

Important Therapeutic Uses
All types of Jvara (Fever), Grahaṇī (Malabsorption syndrome), Gulma (Abdominal
lump), Plīhā Roga (Splenic disease), Kāmalā (Jaundice), Pāṇḍu (Anaemia), Śotha
(Inflammation), Viṣamajvara (Intermittent fever), Jīrṇajvara (Chronic fever), Meha
(Excessive flow of urine), Aruci (Tastelessness), Kāsa (Cough), Śvāsa (Dyspnoea/Asthma),
Āmadoṣa (Products of impaired digestion and metabolism / consequences of Āma), Atīsāra
(Diarrhoea), Mūtrakṛcchra (Dysuria)

Pāṇḍu (Anaemia), Pradara (Excessive vaginal discharge), Kukṣiśūla (Pelvic pain), Kaṭi Śūla
(Lower backache), Yoniśūla (Pain in female genital tract), Aruci (Tastelessness), Śvāsa
(Dyspnoea/Asthma), Kāsa (Cough), Kārśya (Emaciation), Daurbalya (Weakness)

PRADARĀRI LAUHA

(Bhaiṣajyaratnāvalī, Strīrogādhikāra : 70-74)

1. Vatsaka (Kuṭaja) (St. Bk.) 4.800 kg
2. Water for decoction 24.576 l.
reduced to 3.072 l.
3. Samaṅgā (Mañjiṣṭhā) (Rt.) 48 g
4. Śālmalī (Rt.) 48 g
5. Pāṭhā (Rt./Pl.) 48 g
6. Bilva (Rt./St. Bk.) 48 g
7. Mustā (Rz.) 48 g
8. Dhātakī (Fl.) 48 g
9. Aruṇa (Ativiṣā) (Rt.Tr.) 48 g
10. Vyomaka (Abhraka) bhasma 48 g
11. Lauha bhasma 48 g
Special Method of Preparation
Kuṭaja Kaṣāya is first prepared. It is filtered and boiled again to form Rasa Kriyā and
fine powders of all the other drugs are added and mixed well.
Dose
1 to 2 g

Anupāna
Kuśa Mūla Kaṣāya, milk
Important Therapeutic Uses
Pradara (Excessive vaginal discharge), Śūlaroga (Gastric ulcer/Duodenal
ulcer/Colic), Kukṣiśūla (Pelvic pain), Kaṭi Śūla (Lower backache), Dehaśūla (Bodyache),
Daurbalya (Weakness), Agnimāndya (Digestive impairment), Vaivarṇya (Discolouration)

YAKṚDĀRI LAUHA

(Rasendrasārasangraha, Plīhācikitsā: 27-29)
1. Lauha - cūrṇa - bhasma 24 g
2. Abhraka bhasma 24 g
3. Tāmra bhasma 12 g
4. Limpākāṅghri (Nimbū) (Rt. Bk.) 48 g
5. Mṛgājina bhasma 48 g
Note:Antardhūma bhasma of Mṛgājina (burnt ash of Deer's skin) should be taken
Dose

250 to 500 mg
Anupāna
Pippalī Cūrṇa, Honey
Important Therapeutic Uses
Udara (Diseases of abdomen / enlargement of abdomen), Gulma (Abdominal lump),
Kāmalā (Jaundice), Yakṛd roga, Plīhā Roga (Splenic disease), Jīrṇajvara (Chronic fever),
Kāsa (Cough), Śvāsa (Dyspnoea/Asthma), Halīmaka (Chronic obstructive
Jaundice/Chlorosis/Advanced stage of Jaundice), Mandāgni (Impaired digestive fire),
Daurbalya (Weakness)

ROHĪTAKA LAUHA

1. Rohītaka (St. Bk.) 1 Part
2. Suṇṭhī (Rz.) 1 Part
3. Marica (Fr.) 1 Part
4. Pippalī (Fr.) 1 Part
5. Harītakī (P.) 1 Part

7. Āmalakī (P.) 1 Part
8. Mustā (Rz.) 1 Part
9. Citraka (Rt.) 1 Part
10. Viḍaṅga (Fr.) 1 Part
11. Ayaḥ (Lauha) bhasma 10 Parts

Dose
250 mg
Anupāna
Honey
Important Therapeutic Uses
Plīhā Roga (Splenic disease), Yakṛd roga, Śotha (Inflammation)

VIḌAṄGA LAUHA

(Rasendrasārasangraha, Kṛmicikitsā : 26-28)
1. Rasa (Pārada) - śuddha 1 Part
2. Gandhaka - śuddha 1 Part
3. Marica (Fr.) 1 Part
4. Jātīphala (Sd.) 1 Part
5. Lavaṅgaka (Lavaṅga) (Fl. Bd.) 1 Part
6. Śuṇṭhī (Rz.) 1 Part
7. Taṅka (Taṅkaṇa) -śuddha 1 Part
8. Kaṇā (Pippalī) (Fr.) 1 Part
9. Tāla (Haritāla) - śuddha 1 Part
10. Lauha bhasma 9 Parts
11. Viḍaṅga (Fr.) 18 Parts

Dose
500 mg
Anupāna
Kampillaka Cūrṇa, Honey
Important Therapeutic Uses
Agnimāndya (Digestive impairment), Aruci (Tastelessness), Śūla (Colicky Pain), Viṣ
ūcikā (Gastro-enteritis with piercing pain), Arśa (Haemorrhoids), Kṛmi
(Helminthiasis/Worm infestation), Śotha (Inflammation), Jvara (Fever), Hikkā (Hiccup),
Śvāsa (Dyspnoea/Asthma), Kāsa (Cough)

VIḌAṄGĀDI LAUHA

(Bhaiṣajyaratnāvalī; Pāṇḍurogadhikāra : 25-26)
1. Viḍaṅga (Fr.) 1 Part
2. Mustā (Rz.) 1 Part
3. Harītakī (P.) 1 Part
4. Bibhītaka (P.) 1 Part
5. Āmalakī (P.) 1 Part
6. Devadāru (Ht.Wd.) 1 Part
7. Pippalī (Fr.) 1 Part
8. Pippalī mūla (Pippalī) (Rt.) 1 Part

9. Cavya (St.) 1 Part
10. Citraka (Rt.) 1 Part
11. Śuṇṭhī (Rz.) 1 Part
12. Marica (Fr.) 1 Part
13. Āyaścūrṇa (Lauha) bhasma 12 Parts
14. Gomūtra 192 Parts
Special Method of Preparation
Gomūtra and Lauha Bhasma are boiled together to form a thick paste and then fine
powders of other drugs are added and mixed well.
Dose
250 mg
Anupāna
Honey, butter, milk
Important Therapeutic Uses
Kāmalā (Jaundice), Pāṇḍu (Anaemia), Śotha (Inflammation)

SAPTĀMṚTA LAUHA

(Bhaiṣajyaratnāvalī, Śūlarogādhikāra : 83-84)
1. Madhuka (Yaṣṭī) (Rt.) 1 Part
2. Harītakī (P.) 1/3 Part
3. Bibhītaka (P.) 1/3 Part

4. Āmalakī (P.) 1/3 Part
5. Ayoraja (Lauha) bhasma 1 Part
Dose
250 mg
Anupāna
Honey, ghee and milk
Important Therapeutic Uses
Chardi (Emesis), Śūla (Colicky Pain), Amlapitta (Dyspepsia), Jvara (Fever), Mūtrasa
ṅga (Obstruction in urinary tract), Timira (early stage of Catract), Klama (Tiredness without
exertion/Langour), Ānāha (Distension of abdomen due to obstruction to passage of urine and
stools), Śotha (Inflammation)
